A bare `1fr` is `minmax(auto, 1fr)`, and `auto` floors at min-content — so a single unbreakable task title stretched its column to the width of the whole string and took the other six with it. Seven columns of a real week came to three thousand pixels inside an eight-hundred-pixel panel, and most of the month was simply off the side of the page. The zero floor lets a column be narrower than its contents; min-width:0 on the cell lets the ellipsis in the pill actually engage. */ /* Below a certain width a column stops being a day and becomes a sliver: at 1024px the month was down to 55px a cell, which fits a date and nothing else. So each column keeps a floor, and when seven of them no longer fit the panel it is the calendar that scrolls sideways, not the page — the whole month stays reachable either way. */
